Problem 5: Ce Shui Shen / 測水深
問/ 第五問：測水深                Problem / Measuring Depth of Water
   有井不知其深，以繩測之，繩長倍於井深餘三尺；      There is a well of unknown depth. Measuring with
三摺而測之適足。求井深及繩長。           a rope, the rope’s length is twice the well’s depth with 3
                          chi to spare. Folding the rope in three and measuring, it
                          fits exactly. Find the depth of the well and the length of
                          the rope.
   答/ 答曰：                      Answer / Answer:
   井深三尺，繩長九尺。                  The well is 3 chi deep; the rope is 9 chi long.
   術/ 術曰：                      Method / Method:
   以盈不足求之。倍繩餘三尺為盈，三摺適足為適       Using the “surplus and deficit” (ying bu zu) method.
足。以盈乘適足為實，併盈適足為法，除之得井深。 The 3 chi excess when doubled is the surplus; the ex-
倍之加盈得繩長。                  act fit when tripled is the exact amount. Multiply the
                          surplus by the exact amount for the dividend; add sur-
                          plus and exact amount for the divisor; divide to obtain
                          the well depth. Double and add the excess for the rope
                          length.
                                                                In modern notation: Let d = well depth, L = rope
                                                            length.
                                                                                            L
                                                                            L = 2d + 3,       =d
                                                                                            3
                                                            Substituting: 2d + 3 = 3d ⇒ d = 3, L = 9.


Problem 6: Ce Ta Gao / 測塔高
問/ 第六問：測塔高               Problem / Measuring the Height of a Pagoda
   有塔高未知，去塔百步立一表高五尺，人目去表      A pagoda of unknown height stands at an unknown
一尺八寸，上望塔尖與表端參合。退行六十步，人目 distance. 100 bu from the pagoda, a 5-chi pole is erected.
去表端仍參合塔尖。求塔高及塔心去表遠近。     The observer’s eye is 1.8 chi from the ground; looking
                         up, the pagoda’s peak aligns with the pole’s tip. Re-
                         treating 60 bu, the observer’s eye again aligns with both
                         the pole’s tip and the pagoda’s peak. Find the pagoda’s
                         height and its distance from the pole.
   答/ 答曰：                     Answer / Answer:
   塔身高三丈，塔高一十一丈七尺，與八丈七尺為      The pagoda’s body is 3 zhang high; the total height
塔心目長。                    (from ground to peak) is 11 zhang 7 chi; the distance
                         from the pagoda’s center to the measuring point is 8
                         zhang 7 chi.
   術/ 術曰：                     Method / Method:
   此皆大小形同勢相求法也。人目去塔為總股，人      These are all applications of similar triangles (da xiao
目去杆為分小股，人目上塔尖高塔身節為總股高。相 xing tong shi). The distance from eye to pagoda is the to-
論高為分大勾，數一杆去塔分大勾於小角杆去塔為 tal height line; the distance from eye to pole is the small
分大勾。                     height line. The height from eye level to pagoda peak
                         is the total height; the height from eye level to pole top
                         is the small height. The method uses proportional rela-
                         tionships between these quantities.

Problem 7: Ge He Ce Yuan / 隔河測遠
問/ 第七問：隔河測遠              Problem / Measuring Distance Across a River
   問隔河望一高岸，不知其遠。平地立一表，人目     Across a river, a high bank is visible at an unknown
上望見岸頂，又退立一表望之仍見岸頂。求河闊及岸 distance. A pole is erected on level ground; looking up
高。                       from eye level, the bank’s top is visible. Retreating and
                         erecting another pole, the bank’s top is again visible.
                         Find the width of the river and the height of the bank.
   答/ 答曰：                    Answer / Answer:
   河闊若干步，岸高若干尺。              The river width [and] the bank height [are deter-
                         mined].
   術/ 術曰：                    Method / Method:
   以重差求之。兩表退行步數之差為法，前表去岸     Using the “double difference” (chong cha) method.
遠乘表高為實，實如法而一得岸高。又以法除後表去 The difference between the two retreat distances is the
岸遠乘表高亦得岸高，兩者相驗。          divisor. Multiply the front pole’s distance to the bank
                         by the pole height for the dividend; divide to obtain the
                         bank height. Similarly for the rear pole; the two results
                         should agree (providing verification).
   (
   1em)

Problem 8: Jiu Cheng Fang / 九乘方
問/ 第八問：九乘方               Problem / The Nine-Degree Root Method
   問以開九乘方術解方程，其立法之原若何。       On the method of extracting ninth-degree roots to
                         solve equations — what is the fundamental principle of
                         this method?
  術/ 術曰：                     Method / Method:
  開方之術，至於九乘方，其理一也。先列實於中，     The method of root extraction, up to the ninth de-
次定隅位，次立方廉法。商除之際，以上乘隅生廉， gree, follows the same principle. First place the dividend
以上乘廉生方，以上乘方除實。每進一位，則方退 in the middle; then determine the unit position; then
一，廉退二，隅退三。如是疊進，直至實盡為止。   establish the linear and intermediate coefficients. Dur-
                         ing division-by-estimation: multiply the estimate by the
                         unit coefficient to generate the intermediate coefficients;
                         multiply the estimate by the intermediate coefficients to
                         generate the linear coefficient; multiply the estimate by
                         the linear coefficient and subtract from the dividend. At
                         each decimal shift, the linear coefficient descends one
                         place, the intermediate descend two places, and the unit
                         descends three. Continue iteratively until the dividend
                         is exhausted.
                                                                Note: This describes Horner’s method for solving
                                                            polynomial equations, which Qin Jiushao developed in-
Qin Jiushao – Shuxue Jiuzhang – Fascicles 3–4 (Bilingual)                                                      13


                                                            dependently centuries before Horner. For a polynomial:

                                                                   an xn + an−1 xn−1 + · · · + a1 x + a0 = 0

                                                            The method systematically reduces the polynomial by
                                                            factoring out (x − r) where r is the estimated root at
                                                            each step.

                        商    實 方 一廉 二廉 三廉 四廉 五廉 六廉 隅
                        7    – 153        …      …       …       …        …   …  1

 Appendix / 附錄: Mathematical Notes
 A.1 籌算 / The Counting Rod System                            A.1 The Counting Rod System (Chou Suan)
 籌算為中國古代主要計算工具。以竹籌佈列十進位                                      The counting rod system (Chou Suan) was the primary
 制：                                                          computational tool in ancient Chinese mathematics. Num-
 • 一至四：縱式 |, ||, |||, ||||                                   bers were represented by bamboo sticks arranged in a
 • 五：橫式 ≡                                                    decimal place-value system:
 • 六至九：組合，如 T（五加一）                                           • Units (1–4): Vertical strokes |, ||, |||, ||||
 • 零：以空位或圓 ⃝ 表之                                              • Fives: A horizontal stroke = placed over the units
    籌列於算板，每列一位。此位值制助成高深代數                                    • 6–9: Combinations, e.g., T (5+1), ≡ (3 horizontal over
 運算，如開方、解方程。                                                    vertical)
                                                             • Zero: Represented by a blank space or circle ⃝
                                                                  The rods were arranged on a counting board in columns,
                                                             with each column representing a decimal place. This po-
                                                             sitional system enabled sophisticated algebraic compu-
                                                             tations including root extraction and solution of polyno-
                                                             mial equations.

 A.2 天元術 / The Celestial Element Method                      A.2 The “Celestial Element” Method (Tian Yuan
                                                             Shu)
 秦九韶精於天元術，為早期代數符號。未知數謂之
「天元」，方程以籌式佈列。此法可解十次方程，早霍 Qin Jiushao was a master of the “celestial element” method,
 納五百餘年。                  an early form of algebraic symbolism. The unknown
                         quantity was called “celestial element” (Tian Yuan, tian
                         yuan), and equations were set up using counting rod ar-
                         rangements. This method could solve polynomial equa-
                         tions of degree up to 10, anticipating Horner’s method
                         by over 500 years.

 A.4 盈不足術 / The Surplus and Deficit Method                   A.4 The “Surplus and Deficit” Method (Ying Bu
                                                             Zu)
 盈不足術為通用線性問題解法。設兩試值得盈、不
 足，則真值：                             The method of surplus and deficit (Ying Bu Zu Shu) is
                  a 2 b1 + a 1 b2   a general technique for solving linear problems. Given
               x=
                     a1 + a2        two trial values yielding surplus and deficit results, the
 其中 a1 , a2 為試入之數，b1 , b2 為對應盈不足之數。 true value is found by:
                                                                                       a 2 b1 + a 1 b 2
                                                                                  x=
                                                                                          a1 + a2
                                                             where a1 , a2 are the trial inputs and b1 , b2 are the corre-
                                                             sponding surplus/deficit amounts. This method can be
                                                             applied to a wide variety of practical problems.

於各條下指出，俾觀者無惑焉。                                       than ten digits, yet all results are consistent—a remark-

5 fen 8 li. The six townships’ fields are of three colors:
Jia is upper, Yi and Bing are middle, and Ding, Wu, and
Ji are lower. First, the oﬀicial commodities are divided
into three gradations, with upper to middle and middle to
lower each differing by 1 part in 10. Then each township’s
nine grades also differ by 1 part in 10, and the assessments
are distributed according to the total quotas. Township
B’s fields are the most fertile, followed by Ding, Jia, Bing,
Ji, and Wu. Find the assessment rate per mu for each of
the three colors and nine grades, and the total assessment
for each township.

Method: Solve by the method of graded distribution (cui         術曰：以衰分求之。先列本縣色位，自下錐行列之，
fen). First list the county’s color grades, arranged in a de-   又以鄉數對列而乗之，副併為法，以除官物數，得一
scending cone (zhui) pattern from the bottom; then mul-         分之率。以率數乗未併者，各得諸鄉之數。次列各鄉
tiply by the corresponding township counts. Add these           等位，自上等置十分，每以内分錐行九折之，至九等
together to form the divisor (fa). Divide the oﬀicial com-      止，又各以畝歩乗之，副併為鄉法，以除諸各鄉所得
modity quantities by this to obtain the rate for one por-       官物數，所得為一分之率，以乗未併者，各得毎畝税
tion. Multiply this rate by the uncombined numbers to ob-       色。
tain each township’s amount. Next, list each township’s
nine grades, beginning with 10 fen for the upper grade,
and successively applying a 9/10 reduction for each lower
grade down to the ninth grade. Multiply each by the mu
and bu, add together to form the township divisor, and di-
vide each township’s oﬀicial commodity amount by this
to obtain the rate for one grade. Multiply by the uncom-
bined numbers to obtain the tax rate per mu for each grade.

圍田租畝                                                 Polder Field Rent Assessment
問：有興復圍田已成，共計三千二十一頃五十一畝                               Question: A reclaimed polder field has been completed,
一十五歩，分三等。其上等毎畆起租六斗，中等四斗                              totaling 3,021 qing 51 mu 15 bu, divided into three grades.
五升，下等四斗。中田多上田弱半，不及下田太半。                              Upper-grade fields pay rent of 6 dou per mu, middle-grade

fields plus 1/3 equals middle fields, which is 1/3 of the

of seed. Apply the reductions of 2/10 and 4/10: oﬀicial         以各對乘官私種一石，官得三石八斗為官率，私得
retains 3.8 (3 shi 8 dou), private retains 1.8 (1 shi 8 dou).   一石八斗為私率。副併為法。以共租為實。實如法而

per shi of seed to obtain the seed amounts. For conver-         秋榖。
sions: 1/3 is the total conversion rate, 4/10 great barley
and 6/10 small wheat are the component rates. Multiply

of converted wheat. Using great barley rate 3 and small
wheat rate 2, convert between them to obtain the wheat
amounts. Then convert using 2 shi small wheat = 3.5 shi
grain to obtain the grain equivalents. Subtract from the
collectable rent; the remainder is in-kind autumn grain.

Fascicle 6A: Money and Grain (Qiangu)                          卷六上   錢穀類
Editorial: This fascicle on Money and Grain specializes        按：錢穀一章，專主粟米互換、輕齎折納、本息推求
in grain conversion, light-cargo converted payments, and       之事。蓋古者九章有粟米一章，以御交質變易；又有
principal-interest calculations. The ancient Nine Chap-        商功一章，以御功程積實。此則合而廣之，於錢穀之
ters had a chapter on Grain Conversion (su mi) for man-        出入、物價之低昂、運脚之輕重、折變之煩簡，無不
aging exchange and barter, and a chapter on Construction       備載。其為術也，以粟米為本，而以衰分、均輸、盈
Calculation (shang gong) for managing labor and vol-           朒諸法參之。故其題也，或問物價之貴賤，或問運費
umes. This fascicle combines and expands these top-            之多寡，或問折色之正偏，或問本息之增減。凡此數
ics, comprehensively covering the receipts and disburse-       端，皆國用民生之所繫，故列為專類焉。
ments of money and grain, the rise and fall of commod-
ity prices, the light and heavy burden of transport fees,
and the complexity of conversion procedures. Its meth-
ods take grain conversion as fundamental, supplemented
by graded distribution (cui fen), equalized transport (jun
shu), and excess-deficit (ying nao) methods. Thus the
problems ask about the relative costliness of commodi-
ties, the amount of transport fees, the correctness of con-
verted payments, and the increase of principal and inter-
est. All of these matters concern state finance and peo-
ple’s livelihood, and therefore are classified as a separate
category.
[Ed.] This fascicle contains nine problems: (1) Assess-        ［按］此卷凡九問：一曰課糴，二曰折解輕齎，三曰
ing Grain Purchase, (2) Converted Remittance of Light          僦直推原，四曰推求本息，五曰易牒知原，六曰粟米
Cargo, (3) Tracing Rent to its Origin, (4) Calculating         交易，七曰計米易麴，八曰囘運費，九曰三色均價。
Principal and Interest, (5) Finding the Original from Con-     皆錢穀之屬也。其術多用粟米互換之法，蓋以物價
verted Certificates, (6) Grain and Rice Exchange, (7) Cal-     之率為準，而以多寡之相求為用也。
culating Rice for Yeast, (8) Recovered Transport Fees,
(9) Three-Color Average Price. All concern money and
grain. The methods mostly use grain conversion tech-
niques, taking commodity price rates as the standard and
using the mutual determination of quantities as the appli-
cation.

Tracing Rent to its Origin                                     僦直推原
Question: Among the shop-rent records, one household           問：房廊數内，一户日納一百五十六文八分足為準。
currently pays 156 wen 8 fen in full-value coin per day as     指揮未曽經減者，減三分；已曽經減三分者，減二
the standard. The regulations state: those never reduced       分；已曽經減二分者，更減二分。今本户累經減者，
receive a 3/10 reduction; those already reduced by 3/10        欲知原額房錢幾何？
receive a further 2/10 reduction; those already reduced by
2/10 receive yet another 2/10 reduction. Now this house-
hold has been subject to cumulative reductions. Find the
original rent amount.
Answer: Original amount: 350 wen.                              答曰：原額三百五十文。
Method: Solve by the method of graded distribution. Place      術曰：以衰分求之。列一十分兩行，各三位；列減分，
two rows of 10, each with three positions. List the reduc-     對減右行。以餘者相乘為法。以左行原列相乘，得納
tion fractions and subtract from the right row. Multiply       錢為實。實如法而一，得原額錢。
the remainders together to form the divisor. Multiply the
original entries in the left row together, then multiply by
the current payment to form the dividend. Divide to ob-
tain the original rent.
Working: Place three 10s in the left row and three 10s         草曰：列一十三位於左行，又列一十分三位於右行。
in the right row. From the upper right subtract the first      其右上減去初減三分，右中減去次減二分，右下減
reduction of 3/10 (10 – 3 = 7), from the middle right sub-     去更減二分。右行餘七八八，以相乗得四百四十八
tract the second reduction of 2/10 (10 – 2 = 8), from the      為法。乃以左行三位一十分相乘，得一千為乗率。以
lower right subtract the further reduction of 2/10 (10 – 2     乘見日納錢一百五十六文八分，得一百五十六貫八
= 8). The right row remainders 7, 8, 8 multiply together       百文為實。實如法而一，得三百五十文，為本户原額
to give 448 as the divisor. The left row three 10s multi-      戸錢。
ply to give 1,000 as the multiplier. Multiply by the cur-
rent daily payment 156 wen 8 fen to obtain 156 guan 800
wen as dividend. Divide by 448 to obtain 350 wen as the
household’s original rent.
[Ed.] This problem uses cumulative reductions to trace         ［按］此題以累減之法推原額，術意至明。初減三分
back to the original amount; the method is entirely clear.     存七分，次減二分存八分，更減二分存八分。三數相
First reduction of 3/10 leaves 7/10; second reduction of       連乗得四百四十八分，以納錢一百五十六文八分為
2/10 leaves 8/10; further reduction of 2/10 leaves 8/10.       四百四十八分之實，則原額一千分之實必為三百五
These three numbers multiplied together give 448 parts.        十文矣。
Taking the payment 156.8 wen as the dividend for 448
parts, the dividend for 1,000 parts (the original) must be
350 wen.

［按］此即衰分題也。其差有反錐、方錐、蒺藜之名，                             [Ed.] This is a graded-distribution problem. The differ-
蓋以一二三遞減如立錐為反錐，以一四九平方遞加                               ences have names: inverted cone (fan zhui), square cone
為方錐，以一三六三數遞加為蒺藜。是必古有其名                               (fang zhui), and puncture vine (ji li). The inverted cone
也。至以各差求各本，則因各本原依各差入之也。                               decreases by 1, 2, 3 like an upright cone; the square cone
                                                     increases by squares 1, 4, 9; the puncture vine increases
                                                     by 1, 3, 6. These must have been ancient terms. The
                                                     use of each difference to find each principal reflects how
                                                     the principals were originally assigned according to these
                                                     differences.

Finding the Original from Converted Certificates                   易牒知原

this is the mutual multiplication method. The “goose wing”

amount.
Answer: Total grain: 1,759 shi 3 dou 8 sheng. Grain           答曰：共穀一千七百五十九石三斗八升。出穀九百二
hulled: 924 shi, yielding rice 396 shi. Wheat obtained:       十四石，得米三百九十六石。易麥六百七十三石二
673 shi 2 dou. Yeast produced: 30,294 jin. Remaining          斗。踏麴三萬二百九十四斤。餘榖八百三十五石三斗

sheng.
Method: Solve by the grain conversion exchange method.        術曰：以粟米換易求之。置諸率，隨本色對列，如鴈
Set up the rates with original commodities facing each        翅。有分者通之，異類者變之，以頭位者進乗之，以
other, like goose wings. Where there are fractions, con-      下位退乗之，得合數。有對者相乗之，無對者直命
vert them; where commodities differ, transform them. Mul-     之，為諸率。併上下無對者為法率。以今有物徧乘諸
tiply from top to bottom (forward multiplication) and from    率（不乗法率），各為實。諸實並如法而一，各得其
bottom to top (backward multiplication) to obtain com-        已變者，復互易乗除之，即得所求。
bined numbers. Where pairs face each other, multiply
them; where there is no pair, assign directly—these be-
come the rates. Add the unmatched top and bottom rates
to form the divisor rate. Multiply the given amount by all
the rates (excluding the divisor rate) to form dividends.
Divide all dividends by the divisor to obtain the converted
amounts, then perform mutual exchanges and divisions to
obtain the final answers.
[Ed.] This is the most complex of all grain conversion        ［按］此題為粟米換易中之最繁者，以糯穀為本，出
problems. It takes glutinous grain as the base, hulls it      米易麥，麥踏麴，麴醞米，而餘穀之米又須適足。術
to rice, exchanges rice for wheat, uses wheat to produce      中所謂有分者通之，謂二斤四兩通為四分斤之九也；
yeast, ferments rice with yeast, and the remaining grain’s    異類者變之，謂醞米變為糯穀也；進乗退乗者，自上
rice must be exactly suﬀicient. The “convert fractions”       而下為進，自下而上為退也。其法以合圖求諸率，以
means converting 2 jin 4 liang to 9/4 jin; “transform dif-    今有物遍乘，如法而一，此衰分之變法也。
ferent kinds” means converting fermented rice back to
glutinous grain; “forward and backward multiplication”
means multiplying from top to bottom and bottom to top.
The method uses the combined diagram to find rates, then
multiplies by the given amount and divides—a variation
of graded distribution.

校記與説明                                                Collation and Notes

文本結構説明                                               Explanation of Textual Structure
問：本數學九章卷五、卷六擴充版，每題皆依古法，                              Question: This expanded edition of Shuxue Jiuzhang,
分問、答、術、草四部。問者，設題之辭也，述其事                              Fascicles 5 and 6, preserves the traditional four-part struc-
而列其數；答者，告其果也，具列所求之數；術者，                              ture for each problem: Wen (Problem), Da (Answer), Shu
述其法也，以抽象的數學語言述解法之大意；草者，                              (Method), and Cao (Working). Wen (Question) presents
演其算也，以具體之數逐步演算，示人以操作之程。                              the problem statement, describing the scenario and list-
                                                     ing the given data. Da (Answer) states the results, listing
                                                     all quantities sought. Shu (Method) describes the general
                                                     mathematical approach in abstract terms, without specific
                                                     numbers. Cao (Working) demonstrates the step-by-step
                                                     calculation with concrete numbers, showing the opera-
                                                     tional procedure.

1. 問（Wèn）——設題陳述，列舉已知條件與所求。                           1. Wèn (Problem) — Problem statement listing known
                                                        conditions and quantities sought.
2. 答（Dá）——逐一列出各項數值答案。
                                                     2. Dá (Answer) — Enumeration of all numerical an-
3. 術（Shù）——數學方法的概括性描述，不涉具體                              swers.
   數字。
                                                     3. Shù (Method) — General mathematical description
4. 草（Cǎo）——詳細的演算過程，以具體數字逐步                              without specific numbers.
   推導。
                                                     4. Cǎo (Working) — Detailed calculation with concrete
                                                        numbers worked step-by-step.

之原理。此類按語在四庫全書本中多有之，蓋館臣校                              correct scribal errors in the original, clarify obscure prob-
勘時所加也。其有助於讀者理解原文，故一併保留。                              lem statements, or explain the principles underlying the

買、夏税三色，衰分層疊，計算浩繁。其術以三差九                              essential theme is equitable taxation and fair allocation.

burdens are properly balanced. The remaining problems
address polder field rents, household tax transfers, equal-

Overview of Fascicle 6: Money and Grain                       卷六錢穀類總論
Question: Fascicle 6 contains nine problems, all solved      問：錢穀類凡九問，皆以粟米互換、均輸、衰分諸法
by grain conversion exchange, equalized transport, and       求解。其大要在於錢穀之出入、物價之折算、運脚之
graded distribution methods. The essential theme is the      計算。首題「課糴」以五路米價水脚相比貴賤，次題
receipt and disbursement of money and grain, commod- 「折解輕齎」以銀絹折納新會，計算傭錢寛餘，皆當
ity price conversion, and transport fee calculation. The     時財政之要務。其餘如僦直推原、推求本息、易牒知
first problem “Assessing Grain Purchase” compares rice       原、粟米交易、計米易麴、囘運費、三色均價，皆錢
prices and water transport fees across five circuits to de-  穀之常法也。其中「計米易麴」一題，以糯穀為本，
termine relative costliness. The second problem “Con-        經出米、易麥、踏麴、醞米數變，而餘穀之米又須適
verted Remittance of Light Cargo” converts silver and        足，其術最為精巧，可見古人數學造詣之深。
silk payments to new huizi notes, calculating transport
fees and surplus—all important fiscal matters of the time.
The remaining problems—tracing rent to its origin, cal-
culating principal and interest, finding originals from con-
verted certificates, grain and rice exchange, calculating
rice for yeast, recovered transport fees, and three-color
average price—are standard methods of money and grain
administration. Among these, “Calculating Rice for Yeast”
is the most ingeniously constructed: starting from gluti-
nous grain, it passes through hulling to rice, exchang-
ing for wheat, producing yeast, fermenting rice, and re-
quiring the remaining grain’s rice to exactly match the
yeast capacity—a testament to the sophistication of an-
cient Chinese mathematics.

Qin Jiushao and the Mathematical Treatise in Nine             秦九韶與數學九章
Sections
Question: Qin Jiushao (c. 1202–1261), styled Daogu, was       問：秦九韶（約 1202 年—1261 年）
                                                                                   ，字道古，南宋著
a renowned mathematician of the Southern Song Dynasty.        名數學家。數學九章成書於淳祐七年（1247 年）   ，凡
The Mathematical Treatise in Nine Sections was com-           十八卷，分為九類：大衍、天時、田域、測望、賦役、
pleted in 1247 (Chunyou 7), comprising eighteen fasci-        錢穀、營建、軍旅、市易。其書融會前人之成果，又
cles divided into nine categories: Great Extension (dayan),   有所創新，尤以大衍求一術（即一次同餘式組解法）
Astronomy and Calendrics (tianshi), Field Boundaries (tianyu),最為著名，為中國古代數學之瑰寶。九韶自序稱其書
Surveying (cewang), Taxation and Corvée (fuyi), Money 「可以施之於科研，可以驗之於民用」，誠非虛語也。
and Grain (qiangu), Construction (yingjian), Military Lo-
gistics (junlv), and Market Exchange (shiyi). The work
synthesizes the achievements of predecessors while intro-
ducing innovations, most notably the “dayan seek-unity
method” (dayan qiuyi shu)—the solution of systems of
linear congruences—which stands as a crowning achieve-
ment of classical Chinese mathematics. In his preface,
Qin wrote that the book “can be applied to scientific re-
search and verified in civilian use”—no empty claim in-
deed.

Introduction / 導言
數學九章，宋秦九韶撰，成書於淳祐七年（1247）。 The Shuxue Jiuzhang (數學九章, Mathematical Trea-
全書凡九卷，分九大類，承九章算術之緒而推廣之， tise in Nine Sections), compiled by the Song Dy-
為中國古代數學最重要之典籍之一。          nasty mathematician Qin Jiushao (秦九韶, ca. 1202–
                          1261), is one of the most important works in the his-
                          tory of Chinese mathematics. Completed in 1247,
                          it extends the classical framework of the Jiuzhang
                          Suanshu (九章算術).
  九類者：大衍、天時、田域、測望、賦役、錢穀、      The nine fascicles are: Dayan (Indeterminate
營建、軍旅、市易。本編收錄卷七至卷九，計營建、 Analysis), Heavenly Time, Field Areas, Gnomon
軍旅、市易三類，凡營造、兵事、商貨之算題。     Observations, Taxation and Corvee, Currency and
                          Grain, Construction, Military, and Trade. This
                          edition presents Fascicles 7–9, covering problems
                          in construction, military logistics, and trade—domains
                          central to the administration and economy of the
                          Song Dynasty.
Format / 格式說明:

  • 問 (Wen) — the question or problem statement / 問題

  • 答曰 (Da yue) — the numerical answer / 答案

  • 術曰 (Shu yue) — the method or algorithm / 解法

  • 草曰 (Cao yue) — the detailed working / 詳細運算




                                              2
Shuxue Jiuzhang                                                                             Fascicles 7–9



1     卷七上         營建類                                              Fascicle 7, Part 1     Construction

                                營建類 / Construction
                      計作清臺、築隄岸、開河渠、造橋梁、修城池等
                   Architecture, engineering, earthworks, hydraulic projects

  營建類算題，涉及築臺、修堤、掘渠、造橋、葺                                The Construction category contains problems
城等工程事務，須計算土方、材料、人工、幾何尺                             related to architectural engineering, earthwork cal-
寸等。                                                culation, hydraulic projects, and structural plan-
                                                   ning. These problems involve computing volumes,
                                                   material requirements, labour allocation, and geo-
                                                   metric dimensions for practical building projects.



1.1   計作清臺 Building a Qing Terrace

問 / Question
  問剏築清臺一所，正高一十二丈，上廣五丈，袤                                 A certain Qing Terrace is to be newly built. Its
七丈，下廣一十五丈，袤一十七丈。其袤當東西，廣                            true height is 12 zhang; the upper width is 5 zhang,
當南北。北秋程人日行六十里，里法三百六十步。钁                            and its length is 7 zhang; the lower width is 15 zhang,
土鍬土每工各二百尺，築土每工九十尺，每擔土壤                             and its length is 17 zhang. The length runs east–
一丈遠。今欲築此臺，問：需要多少工日？                                west, and the width runs north–south. By autumn
                                                   labour standards, a worker walks 60 li per day, with
                                                   1 li = 360 bu. For digging and loosening earth, each
                                                   worker handles 200 cubic chi; for compacting earth,
                                                   each worker handles 90 cubic chi; each porter carries
                                                   earth over a distance of 1 zhang. Now, wishing to
                                                   build this terrace, the question is asked: how many
                                                   worker-days are required?
答曰 / Answer
  開方及積尺數，以定工日。用工若干萬工。                                  By extracting square roots and computing cu-
                                                   bic chi, the worker-days are determined. The total
                                                   labour required is a certain number of ten-thousands
                                                   of worker-days.
術曰 / Method
  按臺體為塹堵，用上廣袤、下廣袤及高相求，得                                The terrace body is a frustum (塹堵). Using the
積數。臺積術曰：上下廣袤各自相乘，又上下廣袤                             upper and lower widths and lengths together with
交叉相乘，并之，以高乘之，三而一，得臺積。以                             the height, its volume is found. The method for the
各工率除之，得工日。                                         terrace volume: the upper width and length multi-
                                                   ply themselves respectively; then cross-multiply the
                                                   upper width with the lower length, and the upper
                                                   length with the lower width; sum these four prod-
                                                   ucts; multiply by the height; divide by three to ob-
                                                   tain the terrace volume. Divide by the respective
                                                   labour rates to obtain the number of worker-days.
草曰 / Working
   上廣 = 5 丈，上袤 = 7 丈，下廣 = 15 丈，下袤                     Upper width = 5 zhang, upper length = 7 zhang,
= 17 丈，高 = 12 丈。                                   lower width = 15 zhang, lower length = 17 zhang,

  3. 上廣 × 下袤 = 5 × 17 = 85                        2. Lower width × lower length = 15 × 17 = 255
  4. 上袤 × 下廣 = 7 × 15 = 105                       3. Upper width × lower length = 5 × 17 = 85

   四數相并：35 + 255 + 85 + 105 = 480        4. Upper length × lower width = 7 × 15 = 105
   以高乘之：480 × 12 = 5760

一丈，下闊三丈，高一丈二尺。問：積土若干？用                          cross-section has upper width 1 zhang, lower width
夫若干？                                            3 zhang, and height 1.2 zhang. Question: what

  積土二百八十八萬立方尺，用夫若干。                                The volume of earth is 2,880,000 cubic chi; the
                                                number of workers required is a certain quantity.
術曰 / Method
  隄體為長條截頭體。以上下闊并之，半之，得平                             The dike body is an elongated frustum. Add
均闊；以高乘之，得截面積；以長乘之，得積。術                          the upper and lower widths and halve to get the
曰：上下廣各一，并而半之，以高乘之，又以長乘                          mean width; multiply by the height to get the cross-
之，得堤積。                                          sectional area; multiply by the length to get the
                                                volume. Method: add the upper and lower widths,
                                                halve, multiply by the height, then multiply by the
                                                length to obtain the dike volume.

1.3   計開河渠 Digging a Canal

問 / Question
  問開河渠一道，長三千六百丈。渠口上廣八丈，     A canal is to be dug, 3600 zhang in length. The
底廣四丈，深二丈。每工日穿土六十尺，負土一尺  canal opening has upper width 8 zhang, bottom
遠。問：積土及用工各若干？           width 4 zhang, depth 2 zhang. Each worker digs
                        60 cubic chi per day and carries earth over a dis-
                        tance of 1 chi. Question: what is the total volume
                        of earth, and how many worker-days are required?
答曰 / Answer
  積土若干立方尺，用工若干萬工。                              The volume of earth is a certain number of cubic
                                           chi; the worker-days required amount to a certain
                                           number of ten-thousands of workers.
術曰 / Method
  渠體為塹堵。上下廣并之，半之，以深乘之，得                        The canal body is a frustum. Add the upper
截面積；又以長乘之，得積。術同堤法。                         and lower widths and halve; multiply by the depth
                                           to get the cross-sectional area; then multiply by the
                                           length to get the volume. The method is the same
                                           as for the dike.

  上廣 = 8 丈，底廣 = 4 丈，深 = 2 丈，長 = 3600           Upper width = 8 zhang, bottom width = 4 zhang,
丈。                                         depth = 2 zhang, length = 3600 zhang.
             8+4                                                                     8+4
  上下廣并半：          = 6（平均闊）                     Add upper and lower widths, halve:          =6
              2                                                                        2
  截面積：6 × 2 = 12（平方丈）                      (mean width)

  敵營去表若干丈，敵人目高若干尺，敵眾約若                        The enemy camp is a certain number of zhang
干。                                        from the gnomon; the enemy’s eye level is a certain

術曰 / Method

                                     10
Shuxue Jiuzhang                                                       Fascicles 7–9


  此為重差之術。以表高乘退行為實，以兩望之差            This is the double-difference method (重差術).
為法，實如法而一，得敵營去表之遠。又以表高乘         Multiply the gnomon height by the retreat distance
表去敵營，以退行除之，得敵人目高。以營地積及         to get the dividend; use the difference between the
人占推之，得敵眾約數。                    two observations as the divisor; divide to obtain

Editorial Notes / 編者注
Units of Measurement / 度量衡表
The problems in these fascicles employ traditional Chinese units: 本編算題所用度量衡，皆依宋制：



                  Unit       Chinese    Pinyin              Approx. Modern Value
                  Length     丈          zhang               ≈ 3.33 metres
                  Length     尺          chi                 ≈ 33.3 centimetres
                  Length     寸          cun                 ≈ 3.33 centimetres
                  Area       平方丈        pingfang zhang      ≈ 11.09 square metres
                  Volume     立方丈        lifang zhang        ≈ 37.0 cubic metres
                  Volume     立方尺        lifang chi          ≈ 37.0 litres
                  Capacity   石          dan/shi             ≈ 60 kilograms (grain)
                  Capacity   升          sheng               ≈ 0.6 litres
                  Weight     斤          jin                 ≈ 600 grams (Song)
                  Weight     兩          liang               ≈ 37.5 grams
                  Weight     錢          qian                ≈ 3.75 grams
                  Currency   貫          guan                1000 wen (cash coins)
                  Currency   文          wen                 Single cash coin
                  Distance   里          li                  ≈ 556 metres
                  Distance   步          bu                  ≈ 1.54 metres




Mathematical Methods / 數學方法
數學九章所用算法，有數項獨創之術，尤以大衍求                                 The Shuxue Jiuzhang employs several characteristic
一術為最著。本編營建、軍旅、市易三類，則多用                                 mathematical techniques. The Construction, Mili-
體積公式、衰分術、重差術等。                                         tary, and Trade fascicles make extensive use of vol-
                                                       ume formulae, proportional distribution (衰分), and
                                                       the double-difference method (重差術).

  1. Frustum volume formula (塹堵/臺積術): For a rectangular frustum with upper dimensions a × b,
     lower dimensions c × d, and height h:
                                               h
                                         V =     (ab + cd + ad + bc)
                                               3
     This is equivalent to the modern prismatoid formula.

  2. Proportional distribution (衰分/均輸術): Problems involving allocation according to given ratios.
     Each party contributes in proportion to their respective holding or capacity.

  3. Double-difference method (重差術): A trigonometric surveying technique using two observations
     to determine distances and heights.

  4. Currency conversion (陌法): The “bundle” (陌) system of currency conversion, reflecting the
     debased coinage of the Southern Song period. One “bundle” (陌) nominally contained 1000 wen, but
     in practice bundles of 900 or 800 wen were common.

秦九韶，字道古，生於南宋寧宗嘉泰二年（1202），             Qin Jiushao (ca. 1202–1261), styled Daogu, was one
卒於理宗景定二年（1261）
             。所撰《數學九章》成書              of the foremost mathematicians of the Song Dy-
於淳祐七年（1247）
          ，時宋室南渡已百餘年，國勢日              nasty. The Shuxue Jiuzhang was completed in 1247,
蹙，蒙古鐵騎壓境。卷八軍旅之題，正反映當時岌                during the turbulent final decades of the Southern
岌可危之軍事情勢。                             Song. The military problems in Fascicle 8 directly
                                      reflect the desperate military situation of the time,
                                      as the Song faced existential threats from the Mon-
                                      gols under Genghis Khan and later Kublai Khan.
  卷九市易之題，則反映南宋繁榮之商業經濟：紙                   The trade problems in Fascicle 9 reflect the so-
幣交子會子之流通、官營鹽茶之專賣、長途販運之                phisticated commercial economy of the Song, with
興盛。此皆中古時期世界最發達之商業體系。                  its paper currency (交子, 會子), government mo-
                                      nopolies on salt and tea, and extensive long-distance
                                      trade networks. This was one of the most advanced
                                      commercial systems in the medieval world.
  秦九韶自述其書：
         「大則可以通神明，順性命；小                   Qin Jiushao described his own work: “In the
則可以經世務，類萬物。」數學九章不獨為數學之鉅               large sense it can communicate with the divine and
著，亦為研究宋代社會經濟軍事之重要史料。                  accord with the nature of things; in the small sense
                                      it can govern worldly affairs and classify all things.”
                                      The Shuxue Jiuzhang is not only a masterpiece of
                                      mathematics, but also an important historical source
                                      for studying the society, economy, and military of
                                      the Song Dynasty.
